#
# 803: THE RUNG-33 PRESS - THE CARD FINISHES POWER-UP ON THE SECOND CMD1, AND THE CID COMES BACK.
#
# ONE PRESS SPENT, authorized by the operator BEFORE the gate was run. One gate (exit 0, 609 lines),
# one runner (EXIT 0), one `fastboot boot`, nothing flashed, nothing written to storage, no reboot;
# `33e80afe` absent from BOTH device lists at fire time (measured before the gate); capture
# out/stage90/captures/rung33-opcond-20260928-123120-{last_kmsg.txt,gate.log,run.log}, archived by hand,
# last_kmsg 637,213 B sha256 6fe60a12d5b029b34f4bb71fec03c2b38cd2d21f4563ddf677249e848fadecc4.
# Runner's verdict 7 PASS / 1 FAIL / 3 UNREAD COUNTED PER CRITERION, the single FAIL the known
# `seam_sp ... is not sleh_sp-8` criterion: IDENTICAL to rungs 29, 30, 31 and 32, so this press added no
# failure. THE MACHINE STAYED UP: `_post_end_calls = 0x8`, `_post_elapsed` last `0x092a857e` = 8.009 s
# at `_post_cntfrq = 0x0124f800` (19.2 MHz).
#
# (1) THE HEADLINE, AND IT IS 798 SECTION 6's ROW 1. `_opcond_calls = 0x1`, `_opcond_from = 0x40ff8080`,
# `_opcond_arg = 0x40ff8080` (EQUAL to `_opcond_from` - THE DERIVATION IS A NO-OP ON THIS CARD'S OWN
# WORD, exactly as pre-registered), `_opcond_loop_calls = 0x1`, **`_opcond_sends = 0x2`**,
# **`_opcond_busy_seen = 0x1`** - BIT 31 SET, THE CARD REPORTED POWER-UP COMPLETE -
# `_opcond_last_resp = 0xc0ff8080` (= `0x40ff8080 | 0x80000000`, the same OCR with the busy bit now set),
# `_opcond_ticks = 0x340ff` = 213,247 = 11.107 ms. The 100-send bound was NOT approached: the loop
# exited on SEND 2. The arithmetic is exact - 213,247 ticks minus `mmc_ops.c:164`'s `mmc_delay(10)`
# (192,000 ticks) leaves 21,247 ticks = 1,106.6 us FOR TWO COMMANDS, about 553 us each, both inside the
# block's 665.2 us window, which is why both completed. The record named 1.06 s as the worst case; what
# it actually spent is 1.107 ms, 958x less.
#
# (2) AND CMD2 ANSWERED: THE CID COMES BACK AFTER NINE ARMS OF SILENCE. `_cid_complete = 0x1` (was 0x0
# on every arm since rung 16), `_cid_status_any = 0x00000001` (bit 0 SDHCI_INT_RESPONSE, NO ERROR BIT OF
# ANY KIND, against 0x00018000 ERR|TIMEOUT), `_cid_err = 0x0`, `_cid_ticks = 0x49e2` = 18,914 = 985.1 us
# (the command really ran, against 12,772 = 665.2 us of the block's own timeout), `_cid_inhibit_after =
# 0x0` (no longer wedged). THE FOUR RESPONSE WORDS ARE `0x45010053 / 0x44573136 / 0x47014a2f /
# 0xe00bb100` and the `_cid_raw_*` family is the SAME SIXTEEN BYTES SHIFTED ONE, so the two independent
# readings agree. IT DECODES AS A REAL CID: MID `0x45` = 69 = SanDisk, OID `0x01 0x00`, PNM `SDW16G`
# (six ASCII characters), PRV `0x01`, PSN `0x4a2fe00b`, MDT `0xb1`. **793 SECTION 1's ROW 1a IS MET AND
# 793 SECTION 5's FIFTH ROW IS REFUTED IN THE SAME EVENT** - the completion latch fired AND the response
# file filled. **It also retires 790 section 5's and 787 section 6's caveat**: `0x40ff8080` was the
# card's OCR all along, with bit 31 clear because the card had not finished power-up.
#
# (3) AND CMD3 ANSWERED TOO, WITH THE RCA. `_nidx_complete = 0x1`, `_nidx_status_any = 0x00000001`,
# `_nidx_err = 0x0`, `_nidx_ticks = 0x2b22` = 11,042 = 575.1 us, and **`_nidx_resp = 0x00000500` - RCA
# `0x0500`** - with `_nidx_resp_pre = 0x2fe00bb1` (the CID's tail) replaced by it. 795 section 6's
# protocol claim is CONFIRMED in the direction it predicted, and this press supplies the cause it was
# missing: CMD3 failing was always a CONSEQUENCE of CMD2 failing, and neither was the problem - the card
# was not ready, and CMD1 is where readiness is asked for.
#
# (4) THE READING, ONE SENTENCE: every arm from rung 16 through rung 32 was sending CMD2 and CMD3 to a
# card that had not finished power-up, and the ladder's own gate is why. `entry_storage.c`'s CMD2 gate
# tests THE PROBE's response (`c1.resp`, the single CMD1 with argument 0) where the driver tests THE
# LOOP's (`mmc_ops.c:157`). **THE GATE IS NOT MERELY INVERTED; IT IS READING THE WRONG WORD** - the probe
# is a single pass by the driver's own design (`mmc_ops.c:148-150`), so its response says nothing about
# readiness. THE REPAIR IS ONE LINE AND IS NOW MEASURED: gate CMD2 on the loop's result. The 665.2 us
# "timeout" nine rungs chased was never a timeout.
#
# (5) SAFETY, CELL BY CELL. Nothing flashed, nothing written to storage, no reboot; the gate's own last
# line is `booted, never flashed, so no outcome of this run can write to storage.`
# `xnu_live_storage_writes` 0 -> 4 is the same four inherited mode-sequence stores every arm from rung 3
# carries, and NO NEW STORE OF ANY KIND: rung 33's own two bodies have an EMPTY DEVICE SET, asserted out
# of the linked image by `xnu_entry_799` before the press. The 11.107 ms of bus time is the whole of this
# arm's new cost.
#
# (6) ONE DIFFERENCE THIS RECORD DOES NOT EXPLAIN, NAMED RATHER THAN GLOSSED: `_post_end_calls` moved
# `0x7` -> `0x8` and `_post_elapsed` last `0x06df35b6` (6.005 s) -> `0x092a857e` (8.009 s), one more
# post-end call and about two more seconds than every arm since rung 24. The storage probe's own delta is
# 11.107 ms and cannot account for 2 s. OWED A CELL, NOT A SENTENCE.
#
# (7) THE GOAL IS NOT MET. No transfer completes, no filesystem is reached, no mount is made; 794
# section 1's BREADTH GAP is untouched and is now the whole distance (four opcodes, no CMD9 define, no
# data path). TWRP-to-storage STAYS WITHHELD.
#
# 804: THE RUNG-34 ARM - armed-storage-7341f5f6, STAGE90_XNU_STORAGE_PROBE=33 (switch VALUE 33 =
# ordinal rung 34). A BUILD, A PARK AND A RECORD: no press, no device action, no runner, no firer,
# `out/` was rebuilt and a new arm is in it.
#
# THE ARM IS THE CMD2 GATE GIVEN THE WORD THE DRIVER ACTUALLY TESTS, AND IT IS THE RUNG 800 NAMED AS
# THE SEPARATE ONE. 799's own comment above `op_busy` says it in the source: "Gating CMD2 on the loop's
# answer is the driver's semantics and it is a SEPARATE rung - one change per rung is this ladder's own
# discipline". The rung-33 press (803) is what made it a measurement rather than a tidy-up, and it did
# so by showing that the old condition is not merely inverted - it reads the WRONG WORD.
#
# (1) WHAT CHANGED, AND IT IS ONE CONDITION. `entry_storage.c`'s CMD2 gate tested
# `c1.sent != 0 && (c1.resp & ST_MMC_CARD_BUSY) == 0` - the PROBE's response - and now tests
# `c1.sent != 0 && op_busy != 0` - the LOOP's result, the value `st_op_cond_loop` has returned since
# rung 33 and which the rung below computes and throws away with a `(void)` cast. The two arms call the
# same bodies, in the same order, at the same place in the driver's own sequence; what changes is WHICH
# WORD DECIDES WHETHER THE SECOND OF THEM RUNS. The split is `#if >= 33 / #else` INSIDE rung 16's own
# guard, so a value below 33 compiles the old condition byte for byte.
#
# (2) WHY THE OLD CONDITION IS WRONG RATHER THAN BACKWARDS. `mmc_send_op_cond` returns after ONE CMD1
# (`mmc_ops.c:148-150`'s `if (ocr == 0) break;`), so the probe's word is what a card that has not
# finished power-up answers: the OCR with bit 31 CLEAR. `(c1.resp & MMC_CARD_BUSY) == 0` is therefore
# TRUE and the old gate PASSES - it fires on the one reading that means "come back later" and passes on
# the reading that means "not ready yet". That is why `_cid_gated` read 0 on every arm from rung 16
# through rung 32 and why all of them put CMD2 and CMD3 on the bus at a card that had not finished
# power-up. The driver's own condition is `mmc_ops.c:157`'s `if (cmd.resp[0] & MMC_CARD_BUSY) break;` -
# the loop leaves when bit 31 SETS - and that is exactly what `op_busy` is.
#
# (3) THE PRESS THAT MEASURED IT. 803's numbers are the two halves in one log: `_opcond_sends = 2` with
# `_opcond_busy_seen = 1` and `_opcond_last_resp = 0xc0ff8080` against `_cmd1_resp = 0x40ff8080` - THE
# SAME OCR DIFFERING BY EXACTLY THE BUSY BIT - and then `_cid_complete = 1` with a real SanDisk
# `SDW16G` CID that nine arms could never read. So the two words are not two readings of one thing:
# they are the answers to two different questions, and the gate was asking the one the driver does not.
#
# (4) TWO NEW CELLS, PUBLISHED ON BOTH PATHS, AND THEY ARE THE REPAIR MADE READABLE.
# `xnu_live_storage_cid_gate_word` is the word the gate tested, read out of the LOOP's own result
# struct; `xnu_live_storage_cid_gate_busy` is the test's own result printed beside its source, the
# shape `_cmd1_resp_busy` already has four lines above. On a row where the card became ready the word
# differs from `_cmd1_resp` by exactly the busy bit; on a row where the loop never saw the card out of
# reset the two are EQUAL and `_cid_gated` is 1 - which is the row the old gate could not produce at
# all. `c1.sent != 0` is KEPT so `_cid_gated_reason` still separates 1 (CMD1 never reached the bus)
# from 2 (it did, and the loop never saw the card out of reset); reason 2's MEANING is what moves.
#
# (5) NO NEW STORE, NO NEW ADDRESS, NO NEW WIDTH, NO NEW MEGABYTE, NO NEW DEVICE AND NO NEW KEY OF ANY
# DEVICE REGISTER. The rung-33 arm's two bodies keep their EMPTY device sets, `st_cmd_path`'s device
# surface is rung 14's unchanged, `st_send_command` is still called exactly twice from it, and the two
# arms call the same bodies. `xnu_live_storage_writes` does not move.
#
# (6) THE BUILD CLAUSE IS THE READING, NOT THIS BLOCK: `build_entry.sh` gained `xnu_entry_804`, which
# reads THREE numbers out of the LINKED IMAGE in the window between `st_cmd_path`'s call to
# `st_op_cond_arg` and its call to `st_all_send_cid`, and each one FAILS ON THE SPENT RUNG-33 PARK:
#
#   |                              | value 32 (armed-storage-ce2f589c) | value 33 (this arm) |
#   | ---------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ------------------- |
#   | sign-conditioned branches    | **1** (`bge` at 8000eea8)         | **0**               |
#   | loads from the PROBE's word  | **1** (slot [sp, #188])           | **0**               |
#   | loads from the LOOP's struct | **0**                             | **1** ([sp, #300])  |
#   | the two new key strings      | **0 / 0**                         | **1 / 1**           |
#
# **BOTH SLOTS ARE DERIVED AND NOT TYPED**: the probe's word's slot is the `ldr r0, [sp, #N]` that
# feeds `st_op_cond_arg` (both images load [sp, #188] there), and the loop struct's base is the
# `add r1, sp, #N` that materialises `&c1b` before the `st_op_cond_loop` call (both images say 224) -
# so a frame that shifts moves the numbers with it and the check does not go blind. The `bge` is the
# point: `(x & 0x80000000) == 0` is a SIGN test, and the value-32 image carries exactly one inside this
# window at 8000eea8 - the branch that PASSES when the card reports it has NOT finished power-up.
#
# (7) THE CLAUSE REFUSED IN SILENCE TWICE BEFORE IT PRINTED A NUMBER, AND BOTH ARE WORTH THE SENTENCE.
# (a) `(( _off >= gw_loop_off )) && gw_hi=$((gw_hi + 1))` as the last command of a `while` body: a bare
# arithmetic command returns 1 when the comparison is false, and this script runs `set -e`, so the
# first offset BELOW the base ended the build with NO MESSAGE. (b) `x=$(... | grep -oE ...)` and
# `x=$(grep -c ... || true)`'s absence: `grep` exits 1 when it matches nothing and `grep -c` exits 1
# WHEN THE COUNT IS ZERO - which is this clause's GOOD case - so the substitution ended the same way.
# Every pipeline in the clause now carries `|| true` and the emptiness is handled as a refusal with a
# cause. Both are 692's class: a failure reported by the wrong producer, or by none.
#
# (8) CONTAINMENT, MEASURED ON ALL ELEVEN MEMBERS OF THE RUNG BELOW. The value-32 build from THIS SAME
# SOURCE is TEN OF ELEVEN MEMBERS BYTE-IDENTICAL to the SPENT rung-33 park
# `out/stage90/frozen/armed-storage-ce2f589c/` - `xnu_arm_entry.bin` `ce2f589c...` and `stage90-qcdt.img`
# `2c9c2492...` included, i.e. THE ENTRY IMAGE REPRODUCED BYTE FOR BYTE AT VALUE 32 - and the eleventh
# is `xnu_arm_entry-sources.txt`, which records sources BY CONTENT and must move when the tools are
# edited: EXACTLY TWO OF ITS 28 LINES, `build_entry.sh` and `entry_storage.c`, the two files this step
# touched, with the artifact's own hash line identical and the file set identical at 23 files. The
# `#else` branch is what buys this: it is the old condition, verbatim.
#
# (9) THE ARM'S OWN DELTA, MEASURED AGAINST THE SPENT RUNG-33 PARK:
#   entry `.text` (size -A)  5,335,680 -> 5,335,744   **+64**
#   `xnu_arm_entry.elf`      6,732,528 -> 6,732,528   SIZE UNCHANGED (Total 5,934,887 -> 5,934,951, +64)
#   `xnu_arm_entry.bin`      5,552,764 -> 5,552,764   SIZE UNCHANGED, **546,395 bytes differ** - an
#                                                     insertion, not 546,395 edits
#   `stage90-build-config.txt` BYTE-IDENTICAL to rungs 23 through 33 (`6c2b6038...`): no payload switch
#                                                     moved
#   `stage90-qcdt.img`       8,572,928 -> 8,572,928   the boot image the press sends
#   `xnu_arm_entry-config.txt` TWO LINES move: the artifact's own hash, and STORAGE_PROBE 32 -> 33
#
# (10) AND THE ARM IS REPRODUCIBLE: the value-33 build was run twice (once before the containment
# build, once after it) and BOTH RUNS' ELEVEN MEMBERS ARE BYTE-IDENTICAL TO THIS PARK, so the park is
# the build of the tree in front of it and not a lucky copy.
#
# THE PRESS IS THE OPERATOR'S AND NO PRESS IS SPENT OR AUTHORIZED HERE. The arm is ARMED AND NOT
# PRESSED, its park is `out/stage90/frozen/armed-storage-7341f5f6/` with eleven members verified in
# place, and the press path is the two commands `tools/verify_press_ready.sh` prints, run by hand once
# each. **THE GOAL IS NOT MET BY THIS ARM**: no transfer completes, no filesystem is reached and no
# mount is made, and 794 section 1's breadth gap (four opcodes, NO CMD9 DEFINE, no data path) is the
# whole distance - CMD9's `MMC_RSP_R2` is the next command in the driver's order and the breadth gap's
# first body, and THIS rung is what makes it reachable. So 「让os可以正常启动并且挂载存储」 is not
# reached and **TWRP-TO-STORAGE STAYS WITHHELD**.
#

#
# 808: THE CITATION THE TREE GREW OUT FROM UNDER - a check whose baseline is GIT. NO ARM MOVED: this
# step added `tools/check_line_citations.py`, wired it into `make check`, and appended this block.
# `out/` was NOT written - nothing under it is newer than the step's start, the park
# `out/stage90/frozen/armed-storage-7341f5f6/` verifies 11 file(s), and all ELEVEN live members'
# sha256 values agree with the `armed-storage-7341f5f6` lines above. The arm is still ARMED AND NOT
# PRESSED and **THE PRESS IS THE OPERATOR'S**.
#
# WHAT IT CLOSES: 802 section 7 owed "a check that every site citing a line number still resolves",
# and named the drift by hand - the ladder's CMD2 gate had moved from `entry_storage.c:4785` to the
# line in use now, and the single CMD1 call from the line 798 named to the one in use now. (THE TWO
# NEW NUMBERS ARE DELIBERATELY NOT WRITTEN HERE. See the paragraph below.)
#
# THE OBVIOUS CHECK IS "THE CITATION RESOLVES", AND IT IS WRONG TWICE OVER, BOTH MEASURED BEFORE A
# LINE WAS WRITTEN: (a) 2050 of 2950 citations under `docs/experiments/` name vendor sources - the
# Sdhci, Mmc-ops and cache sources that live in the vendor checkout and are NOT tracked here - so a
# rule against a file that does not resolve would refuse two thousand CORRECT citations and be
# turned off within a step (m775's shape); and (b) resolving a bare basename to the first hit made an
# older prototype read `build.sh:389` as the ARCHIVED snapshot's copy, 165 lines long, and report it
# past the end - **a one-value-two-definitions defect committed by the checker itself**.
#
# SO THE BASELINE IS GIT. A tool that stores its own expected values is a self-written record, and
# `a self-written record is not a constraint` is this project's rule about those. The ground truth
# already exists: the COMMIT THAT WROTE THE CITATION. One `git log -p --reverse -U0` walk per citing
# file dates every token in it, and the cited file at that commit yields what the citation MEANT.
# `MOVED` - the text it named is now at exactly ONE other line - REFUSES and PRINTS the new line.
# `MOVED-AMBIGUOUS` (two or more candidates) and `RE-WRITTEN` and `UNDATED` and `SITE-ABSENT` and
# `PAST-END` REPORT; **guessing among duplicates is the defect the check exists to catch, and 147 of
# `entry_storage.c`'s 4068 distinct line texts occur more than once, so the class is real** - the
# first cut read a duplicated comment fragment as "moved to line 1". `NOT-IN-REPO` is passed over BY
# NAME. A bare basename that two LIVE roots hold is `AMBIGUOUS-PATH` and refuses.
#
# WHAT REFUSES IS ONLY THE PROSE THAT PRINTS FOR THE LIVE ARM - the `rung_para` line and the
# `entry_conseq+=` lines for the rung the artifact's own switch record names - because that is what
# the operator reads at press time. Other rungs' paragraphs, every `#` comment, THIS FILE and
# `docs/experiments/` are REPORTED, not refused: they are historical records and are superseded
# rather than rewritten, the same split `tools/check_count_citations.py` makes.
#
# **AND THIS BLOCK THEREFORE CITES NO LINE NUMBER, WHICH IS DELIBERATE RATHER THAN STYLISTIC.** A
# record is checked by this tool like anything else, and a citation written here that the tree later
# grows past would appear in the check's report the day it drifts - a record that reports itself is
# noise in the very list it is trying to keep meaningful. Where a number is needed, the block above
# names the SITE and lets the check or a reader read the line.
#
# THE READING: 213 citations over the press path (`tools/verify_press_ready.sh` and this file) -

# 809: THE REMAINING DISTANCE, COUNTED - CMD9 needs no new mechanism, and the wall is CMD8. A

# THE RECORD SAID THE DISTANCE WAS "four opcodes, NO CMD9 DEFINE, no data path". THAT IS RIGHT ABOUT
# THE OPCODE AND MISLEADING ABOUT THE MECHANISM, and the difference changes what the next rung costs.
#
# THE DRIVER'S OWN INIT ORDER, read out of `mmc_init_card` and out of the driver's own header, which
# prints each command's access type beside its number: CMD2 (bcr R2, arg 0) -> CMD3 (ac R1,
# `RCA << 16`) -> `mmc_set_bus_mode(PUSHPULL)` -> **CMD9 (ac R2, `card->rca << 16`)** ->
# `mmc_decode_csd` -> `mmc_decode_cid` -> **CMD7 (ac R1, `RCA << 16`)** -> **CMD8 `SEND_EXT_CSD`,
# printed as `adtc` - 512 BYTES ON THE DAT LINES** -> CMD6, printed as `R1b`, several times.
#
# AND ALL THREE OF CMD9's INGREDIENTS ALREADY EXIST IN THIS LADDER, BECAUSE CMD2 BUILT THEM. The
# driver's `mmc_send_csd` is, for a native host, `mmc_send_cxd_native(host, card->rca << 16, csd,
# MMC_SEND_CSD)`, and that function's whole body sets `flags = MMC_RSP_R2 | MMC_CMD_AC` - **no data
# lines** - waits, and copies FOUR response words out. The ladder already has:
#   * the flag set - `ST_MMC_RSP_R2`, `= PRESENT|136|CRC`, defined at `:2170`;
#   * the mapping - `ST_MMC_RSP_136` to `ST_SDHCI_CMD_RESP_LONG`, with a `_Static_assert` that the
#     command word for CMD2 with R2 is the value the ladder already sends;
#   * **THE 136-BIT ASSEMBLER**, at `:3531-3544` and again at `:3639-3654` - and this is the
#     non-obvious part, which is why it is written down: the response is NOT four words read in order
#     from `RESPONSE 0x10`. The driver reads `+12, +8, +4, +0`, shifts each left by 8, and ORs in the
#     high byte of the NEXT word down, under the comment "CRC is stripped so we need to do some
#     shifting" (`sdhci.c:1165-1172`). The ladder's copy is that pattern exactly, and it is how rung
#     33 read a real SanDisk CID. **A builder who read the four words in order would get a wrong
#     128-bit value that still LOOKS like a CSD: no error, no fault, just a number that decodes to
#     nonsense.** So a new arm must CALL that assembler and not re-derive it;
#   * the argument - `ST_MMC_RCA_1 = 0x00010000u`, `_Static_assert`ed, and already sent as CMD3's
#     argument at three sites.
# **SO RUNG 35 IS ONE OPCODE DEFINE, ONE CALL AND ITS CELLS - NOT A MECHANISM.**
#
# AND THE BUS-MODE TRANSITION THE DRIVER MAKES BETWEEN CMD3 AND CMD9 IS NOT A DEVICE ACT.
# `mmc.c:1412` calls `mmc_set_bus_mode(host, MMC_BUSMODE_PUSHPULL)` under the comment "quit open
# drain mode", and **NEITHER `sdhci.c` NOR `sdhci-msm.c` REFERENCES `ios->bus_mode` ANYWHERE - zero
# occurrences, checked by name over both files.** `sdhci_do_set_ios` reads clock, power mode, bus
# WIDTH and vdd; never the bus mode. So rung 35 must NOT invent a store for it. What that means for
# the card's own open-drain state is a question for a press and is named here rather than assumed.
#
# CMD7 IS THE SHAPE OF CMD3: `_mmc_select_card` is `MMC_RSP_R1 | MMC_CMD_AC` with `card->rca << 16`,
# and the ladder already sends R1 commands from four sites. NO NEW MECHANISM.
#
# THE WALL IS CMD8, AND IT IS THE FIRST DATA COMMAND. `mmc_send_ext_csd` is `mmc_send_cxd_data(...,
# MMC_SEND_EXT_CSD, ext_csd, 512)`, which builds a full request with a `struct mmc_data` (`blksz =
# 512`, `blocks = 1`, `MMC_DATA_READ`, a scatterlist) - and the header prints the access type in the
# definition itself: `adtc`, the one type this ladder has never sent. **AND THE LADDER SAYS SO IN ITS
# OWN SOURCE**, long before this step: a comment beside the command-word builders states that there
# is no data-path register here at all - `BLOCK_SIZE`, `BLOCK_COUNT` and `TRANSFER_MODE` are absent -
# and another adds that `TRANSFER_MODE` is not written because `sdhci_send_command` returns on its
# first line for a data-less command.
#
# CMD6 IS R1b, THE BUSY RESPONSE, WHOSE MAPPING EXISTS AND HAS NEVER BEEN PASSED.
# `ST_SDHCI_CMD_RESP_SHORT_BUSY` and the `ST_MMC_RSP_BUSY` arm of the flags macro are both in the
# file, and **no call site has ever passed `ST_MMC_RSP_BUSY`** - measured, zero uses outside the
# mapping. That is the shape of a value nothing reads, and it is stated here as a BOUNDARY rather
# than as a defect: the mapping came with the flags and no rung has needed it. R1b means the card
# drives DAT0 busy after the response, so the rung that sends CMD6 is the rung that exercises it -
# a SECOND new mechanism, in the same family as the data path and not smaller.
#
# THE DISTANCE COUNTED: CMD9 -> CMD7 -> **the data path, which begins at CMD8** -> CMD6 (R1b) -> bus
# width and bus speed -> CMD16/17/18 -> and then a filesystem above the block device. TWO
# CONSEQUENCES FOR THE OPERATOR: the next two rungs are the CHEAPEST this ladder has left and the
# third is not; and **「进入操作系统」 and 「挂载存储」 ARE GATED BY DIFFERENT THINGS** - nothing in this
# list puts the OS on the machine. This ladder is the path to a mountable volume. This block measures
# one of the two and does not claim the other.

# 810: THE WORD ORDER THE DRIVER OWNS, MADE A REFUSAL INSTEAD OF A SENTENCE. A CHECK, and it is the

# 809 SECTION 4 WROTE THE TRAP DOWN AND LEFT IT AS ADVICE ("a new arm must CALL that assembler and not

#
# AND MEASUREMENT CORRECTED THE RECORD'S NUMBER. 809 named TWO sites - "the 136-bit assembler at
# `:3531-3544` and again at `:3639-3654`". The scanner finds SIX response-reading functions and 32
# accesses, in THREE shapes: `st_send_command` `:2572` (2 - the offset-0 word, under `#if >= 12` and
# again under the `#else`); **`st_all_send_cid` `:3333` (7) and `st_resp_before` `:3572` (7), the
# four-word assembly**; `st_set_relative_addr` `:3921` (4) and `st_cmd3_noresp` `:4071` (4), the word-0
# pair; and `st_cmd3_noidx` `:4186` (8), the four raw words as a freshness witness. **THE ARITHMETIC IS
# DEFINED FIVE TIMES, NOT TWICE** - and `:3995`'s own comment states the intent out loud: "so that this
# reading, rung 17's `_cid_resp0` and rung 18's `_rb_resp0` are one arithmetic at three times rather
# than three derivations that agree." That is `one value, two definitions` caught in the act of being
# careful: five definitions, currently in agreement, and the next rung is the one that adds a sixth.
#
# THE GROUND TRUTH IS THE VENDOR'S OWN TWO EXPRESSIONS, `external/android_kernel_xiaomi_cancro/drivers/
# mmc/host/sdhci.c:1162-1172`: `SDHCI_RESPONSE + (3-i)*4` - **word 3 first**, 12/8/4/0; `<< 8` on each;
# and `if (i != 3)` with `-1` - **the CRC-stripped byte comes from the address ONE BELOW the word, and
# the LAST word takes no byte at all.** The rules are read off those and not invented: a 32-bit offset
# in {12,8,4,0}, an 8-bit offset in {11,7,3}, the descending order within one stimulus segment (broken
# at every `st_send_command`), and the byte one below its own word.
#
# WHY IT CANNOT BE LEFT TO A READING: read the words ascending and the response is reversed; drop the
# byte-below OR and each word loses 8 bits; OR a byte onto the last word and its low byte is stale.
# EVERY ONE OF THEM PARSES, DECODES AND PUBLISHES - no fault, no error, no cell that says "wrong".
#
# THE REFUSALS ARE DEMONSTRATED, NOT ASSERTED: five mutations applied TO COPIES OF THE FILE, never to
# the tree, each refused in place. Ascending first pair -> ASCENDING + PAIR; the byte taken from `+12u`
# -> OFFSET + PAIR; **the LAST word OR'd with `+3u` -> PAIR** ("the byte at + 3u is OR'd onto the word
# at + 0u"), which is `i != 3` made structural, because an OR onto the offset-0 word has no offset -1
# to name; `+13u` -> OFFSET + PAIR; one word read twice in a segment -> REPEAT. And the UNMUTATED FILE
# IS CLEAN, so the five refusals are the mutation and not the file.
#
# TWO NARROWINGS, EACH BY A MEASURED COUNTER-EXAMPLE AND NOT IN ADVANCE - the first two runs refused
# FOUR CORRECT SITES, and THE RULE MOVED, NOT THE CODE:
#   * COMMENTS ARE STRIPPED FIRST. A definition is recognised by the last `name(...)` before a `{`, and
#     this file's comments are full of parenthesised prose, so a comment ending in a brace was read as
#     the definition of a function called `29` (the text `rung 29 (` is enough) - which reset the
#     enclosing body and produced two PAIR refusals against correct code at `:3539` and `:3647`.
#   * A PREPROCESSOR DIRECTIVE ENDS A SEGMENT. `st_send_command` reads the offset-0 word at `:2771`
#     under `#if >= 12` and again at `:2776` under the `#else`; those are ALTERNATIVES, and only one is
#     ever compiled, so a repeated offset across a `#if`/`#else` boundary is not a second reading of
#     one address. Both narrowings are named in the tool's header with the site that produced them.
#
# THE TOOL'S OWN WIDTH IS TESTABLE: `--selftest` holds NINE fixtures - the three shapes this tree holds,
# and each of the five wrong ways - and returns exit 2 for a failed selftest, reserved for "the check's
# own width is wrong", distinguishable from the exit 1 that means "the tree has a defect". The six sites
# are PRINTED BY NAME on every run, so the check reports what it read and not only what it refused.
#
# WHAT IT DOES NOT DO. It does not say the card answers CMD9 - whether a CSD comes back is a press's
# answer. It does not make the five definitions one definition: it makes them AGREE and keeps them
# agreeing, which is the strongest thing a check can do to a value defined five times, and the collapse
# belongs to the rung that adds the sixth. It has nothing to say about citations or counts, which other
# checks own. `make check` exit 0, 10.4 s.
#
# WHAT IT CHANGES FOR THE NEXT RUNG. CMD9 (`SEND_CSD`, `ac R2`) is the next rung, and 809 measured that
# it needs no new mechanism. What was a sentence in 809 is NOW A REFUSAL IN `make check`: an arm that
# builds the CSD in ascending order, forgets the byte below, or ORs the last word FAILS THE BUILD
# instead of publishing a plausible number. That was the only part of CMD9 that could fail silently.

# **AND A READING THAT IS NEW, AND THAT CHECKED THE ASSEMBLY FROM OUTSIDE THE IMAGE.** The 128-bit CID
# assembled by the driver's word-3-first arithmetic is `450100534457313647014a2fe00bb100`. The vendor's
# own decode path - `mmc_decode_cid` (`mmc.c:83`), cases 2/3/4 at `:120`, which is the eMMC v4 path this
# card takes - reads `card->cid.serial = UNSTUFF_BITS(resp, 16, 32)`, i.e. bits [47:16]. That is
# **0x4a2fe00b**, and `4a2fe00b` is THIS PHONE'S OWN SERIAL NUMBER: `adb devices` lists it as the
# serial, and the phone reports `ro.serialno = ro.boot.serialno = 4a2fe00b` and
# `ro.boot.bootdevice = msm_sdcc.1`. So the bootloader read the same field out of the same card,
# through its own code, before this image existed - and the value the LADDER assembled agrees with it.
# **That is an independent cross-check of the quantity 810 just made a build refusal**, and it is the
# strongest statement available about the arithmetic short of a second card: a reader who assembled the
# four words in the wrong order would not reproduce the phone's own serial number. The product-name
# field decodes to `DW16G` under the SanDisk manufacturer id `0x45`, which is 803's `SDW16G`.
#
# THE RETURN IS THE SAME RETURN. The device came back **28 s** after `fastboot boot` - and the rung-33
# press measured **28 s** on the same criterion, the rung-32 press **26 s**. The runner's own note
# gives the two candidates for this arm as ~28 s if the SoC's watchdog countdown fired and ~90 s if the

# added no failure**, and the three UNREADs are the same `SLOT_NULL` absences the last press reported.
#

# **TWRP-TO-STORAGE STAYS WITHHELD**. The arm is SPENT. **THE NEXT RUNG IS CMD9** (`SEND_CSD`,

# 812: THE RUNG-35 ARM - armed-storage-6cd6fae8, STAGE90_XNU_STORAGE_PROBE=34 (switch VALUE 34 =
# ordinal rung 35) - CMD9, `SEND_CSD`, the driver's first command above CMD3. A BUILD, A PARK AND A
# RECORD: no press, no device action, no runner, no firer; `out/` was rebuilt and a new arm is in it.
#
# (1) WHAT CHANGED. ONE new command body in `src/entry/entry_storage.c`, `st_send_csd`, guarded by
# `#if STAGE90_XNU_STORAGE_PROBE >= 34`, plus its ONE call in `st_cmd_path` on the line below rung 21's
# CMD3. The body is the ladder's second 136-bit command and its first with a NON-ZERO argument: it puts
# the driver's own word `0x0909` (opcode 9, `RESP_LONG 0x01 | CRC 0x08`, no INDEX) on the bus with
# argument `ST_MMC_RCA_1` and flags `ST_MMC_RSP_R2`, opens and closes the same five-bit `INT_ENABLE 0x34`
# window every command body above it opens, publishes the same per-command cell set, and assembles the
# 136-bit response in the driver's word-3-first order. A new opcode define, a new `_Static_assert` on
# the folded word, and the ladder's own bound `> 33` -> `> 34` in `entry_storage.c`; `build_entry.sh`
# gained the `xnu_entry_812` clause. **EVERY GUARD IS A WHOLE-`#if` ALTERNATIVE**: at value 33 and below
# the preprocessor emits the file it emitted before, character for character.
#
# (2) THE ARGUMENT, AND IT IS THE ONE THING THIS RUNG HAD TO SETTLE BEFORE IT COULD BE BUILT. CMD9's
# argument is `card->rca << 16` with `card->rca = 1` ASSIGNED BY THE DRIVER at `mmc.c:1400` - eight
# lines above `mmc_set_relative_addr`, which sends it as `card->rca << 16` (`mmc_ops.c:203`) - and the
# driver's own comment at `mmc.h:38` says the same three things: `MMC_SEND_CSD 9` / `ac [31:16] RCA R2`.
# **AN MMC CARD DOES NOT RETURN AN RCA TO THE HOST.** `mmc.h:32` gives CMD3 the response `R1`, which is
# the 32-bit CARD STATUS: `R1_CURRENT_STATE` is its bits 12:9 (`mmc.h:141`), `R1_READY_FOR_DATA` its bit
# 8 (`mmc.h:142`), and there is NO RCA FIELD AT ANY OFFSET. (An SD card returns one in R6, and even
# there the field is bits 31:16.)
#
# **SO A CLAIM THREE DOCUMENTS CARRIED IS FALSE, AND IT IS THE `one value, two definitions` CLASS.** 811
# section 3 read `_nidx_resp = 0x00000500` as "RCA 0x0500" - i.e. it read bits 15:0 as a field that is
# not one - and 803's block and the `_nidx_resp` sentence in `mi4-the-cid-serial-is-the-phone-serial`
# carry the same reading. The SAME WORD under the vendor's own two macros is
# `R1_READY_FOR_DATA` set with `R1_CURRENT_STATE = 2`, and 2 is `R1_STATE_IDENT` (`mmc.h:149`) - the
# state a card is in AFTER CMD2 and BEFORE a CMD3 it has ACCEPTED. **The ladder's own published cell says
# it too**: `_nidx_state` = `(c3.resp >> 9) & 0xF` = **2** in the committed rung-34 capture, and the
# ladder's own `_nidx_ready` = `(c3.resp >> 8) & 1` is the vendor's `R1_READY_FOR_DATA` bit. Two
# readings of one word, and the one with no basis in either response format is the one the record used.
#
# (3) WHY THAT MATTERS MORE THAN A CORRECTION. CMD9 is legal in STBY and TRAN and not in IDENT - and
# this ladder has never had a CMD3 the card took. So the arm's frontier is NOT CMD9's mechanism (809
# measured that it needs none: the R2 flags, the 136-bit assembler and the RCA argument all exist
# because CMD2 built them); it is that no arm has yet moved the card out of IDENT. **THIS RUNG DOES NOT
# ASSUME OTHERWISE AND DOES NOT GATE ON IT EITHER - IT PUBLISHES IT.** `RESPONSE 0x10` is read BETWEEN
# CMD3 and CMD9 - a moment no rung has read at, which is rung 18's own shape (`st_resp_before`) - and
# published raw and decoded as `_csd_pre_resp` / `_csd_pre_state` / `_csd_pre_ready` /
# `_csd_pre_illegal`. `_csd_pre_state = 3` or `4` is the reading that says the card was listening; 2 says
# it was not, in the same log that reports what CMD9 did with it. **A gate that could not be honest
# about its own precondition would be worse than no gate**: it would turn "the card is not ready for this
# command" into "the command failed", which is the conflation 804's own repair is about one rung down.
#
# (4) THE FOUR WORDS ARE DECODED WITH THE VENDOR'S OWN OFFSETS, WHICH MAKES A CSD ANSWER CHECKABLE FROM
# OUTSIDE THIS IMAGE. `mmc_decode_csd` (`mmc.c:147`) reads `csd->structure = UNSTUFF_BITS(resp, 126, 2)`
# FIRST and returns `-EINVAL` from `mmc.c:162` unless it is non-zero, then
# `csd->mmca_vsn = UNSTUFF_BITS(resp, 122, 4)` (`mmc.c:165`); on the eMMC v4 path this card takes
# (`mmc.c:110`, the same `case 2/3/4` that carries the CID's 32-bit serial) the CSD is v1.2, so
# **`_csd_structure` must read 1 and `_csd_mmca_vsn` must read 4** - two values this ladder did not
# supply and could not have chosen, which is the same free cross-check the CID's `serial` gave against
# `ro.serialno` (811 section 4, 0x4a2fe00b). The rest is `mmc.c:165-196` verbatim: `cmdclass` at 95:84,
# `read_blkbits` at 83:80, `C_SIZE` at 73:62 (which spans the w2/w1 boundary and is written as the
# vendor's own two-piece expression), `C_SIZE_MULT` at 49:47, and `capacity = (1 + C_SIZE) <<
# (C_SIZE_MULT + 2)`. **`_csd_capacity_blocks` IS EXPECTED TO BE A PLACEHOLDER ON THIS CARD AND THAT IS
# STATED RATHER THAN LEFT TO BE MISREAD**: a 12-bit C_SIZE cannot express 16 GB, which is why
# `mmc_get_ext_csd` (`mmc.c:201`) says in the vendor's own words that high-capacity cards store a
# "magic" size in the CSD and take the real one from the EXT_CSD - which is CMD8, 809's wall, named one
# rung early by arithmetic.
#
# (5) THE BUILD CLAUSE IS THE READING, NOT THIS BLOCK: `build_entry.sh` gained `xnu_entry_812`, which
# reads the arm out of the LINKED IMAGE. Every number in it was MEASURED before it was written, and the
# whole clause is a statement about the ARTEFACT that 810's check cannot make about the source:
#
#   r0 = **9**      the opcode, `SEND_CSD` (mmc.h:38) - an arm whose opcode moved is not CMD9 at all
#   r1 = **65536**  `ST_MMC_RCA_1` - the value the DRIVER assigns, and the one a reader is likeliest
#                   to get wrong (see (2)); 0 would be CMD9 addressed to no card, and a value taken
#                   from RESPONSE 0x10 would be an argument that came from nowhere
#   r2 = **7**      `ST_MMC_RSP_R2` = `PRESENT | 136 | CRC`, the driver's own flags for CMD9
#                   (`mmc_ops.c:224`'s `MMC_RSP_R2 | MMC_CMD_AC`, with `MMC_CMD_AC` = 0). 3 would be
#                   rung 31's CRC-dropped word; 5 would be CMD3's 48-bit word
#
#   device accesses IN PROGRAM ORDER, distinct:
#     f9824910:ldr  RESPONSE 0x10   the PRECONDITION read, before the window opens
#     f9824934:str  INT_ENABLE 0x34 the window OPENS
#     f9824934:ldr  INT_ENABLE      `_csd_ena_held`
#     f9824938:ldr  SIGNAL_ENABLE
#     f982491c:ldr  RESPONSE 0x1C   WORD 3 - `sdhci.c:1167`'s `(3-i)*4`, the driver's FIRST word
#     f9824918:ldr  RESPONSE 0x18   WORD 2
#     f9824914:ldr  RESPONSE 0x14   WORD 1
#     f982491b:ldrb RESPONSE 0x1B   the CRC byte ONE BELOW word 3
#     f9824917:ldrb RESPONSE 0x17   ONE BELOW word 2
#     f9824913:ldrb RESPONSE 0x13   ONE BELOW word 1
#     f9824930:ldr  INT_STATUS      `_csd_status_post`
#     f9824924:ldr  PRESENT_STATE   `_csd_ps_after`
#   and there is **NO BYTE AT 0x0F** - `sdhci.c:1168`'s `if (i != 3)` gives the LAST word no byte - which
#   is a property visible in an address list and nowhere else. A byte at 0x0F would be a stale byte OR'd
#   onto word 0; 0x0B or 0x1F would be the pairing off by one; an ASCENDING order (0x10, 0x14, 0x18,
#   0x1C) would be the same eight accesses and the 128-bit value with its four words reversed. **All
#   three parse, decode and publish a plausible CSD-shaped number with no fault anywhere**, which is why
#   the SET and the ORDER are both asserted and not only the set.
#
#   AND THE COUNT `f9824910:ldr=2` IS THE OTHER HALF: `RESPONSE 0x10` is read TWICE - once as the
#   precondition, once as word 0 - and a compiler that COMMONED the two loads would leave a count of 1
#   with the order list still reading correctly, publishing a card state taken at the wrong moment with
#   nothing on the wire to show it. **The key strings `_csd_pre_state`, `_csd_op`, `_csd_structure` and
#   `_csd_mmca_vsn` are asserted present** (the precondition is a register and not a load; the two
#   decodes are the self-check), the image side is asserted EMPTY, and `st_cmd_path` is asserted to call
#   `st_send_csd` exactly once.
#
# (6) 810's CHECK FOUND THE SIXTH COPY WITHOUT BEING TOLD, AND THAT IS THE POINT OF IT.
# `tools/check_response_word_order.py` now reports **seven response-reading functions and 0
# divergences**, with `st_send_csd()` at `src/entry/entry_storage.c:4534`, 8 accesses - so the sixth
# definition of that arithmetic AGREES with the five above it by construction rather than by review.
#
# **AND THE LINE NUMBER IN THIS BLOCK WAS CORRECTED WHILE WRITING IT, WHICH IS A DEFECT OF ITS OWN
# CLASS.** This block first said `:4460`; the doc's section 6 first said `:4475`; the tree says
# `:4534`. Three numbers for one site, all from the same tool, is `one value, two definitions` reached
# through a TOOL rather than through a response register. The cause is FIXED, not documented: the tool
# printed the joined STATEMENT's first line, and a body that follows `#if STAGE90_XNU_STORAGE_PROBE >= N`
# and its comment is joined with them, so it printed the top of that body's own comment block. It now
# computes the definition line (a newline count to the name group, exact, and it survives the file
# growing above the function). Every one of the seven lines it printed moved: `2612 3373 3612 3961 4111
# 4226 4475` -> `2630 3432 3672 4023 4146 4310 4534`, each now equal to the definition it names, and
# the nine-fixture `--selftest` is unchanged at ok. **A printed `path:line name()` is a citation, and a
# citation that does not resolve is not one.**
#
# **AND THE SAME MEASUREMENT OVER THE VENDOR TREE CAUGHT EIGHT MORE, WHICH IS A GAP IN `make check` AND NOT
# ONLY IN THIS BLOCK.** Every citation this step makes into `external/android_kernel_xiaomi_cancro/` was
# re-measured against those files: `mmc.c:1416` -> **1420** (`mmc_send_csd`'s call, the block's own
# premise), `mmc.c:150` -> **147** (`mmc_decode_csd`'s definition), `mmc.c:152` -> **162** (the structure
# check's `-EINVAL`), `mmc.c:165-186` -> **165-196** (the decode body's range), `mmc.c:195` -> **201**
# (`mmc_get_ext_csd`'s definition), `sdhci.c:1165` -> **1167** (`(3-i)*4`), `sdhci.c:1169` -> **1168**
# (`if (i != 3)`), and "eight lines above it" -> **nine** (1400 to 1409). They are corrected in the SOURCE
# COMMENTS and in the readiness narration as well as here, because the same sentence was written in four
# places. `mmc.c:1400`, `mmc.c:1409`, `mmc.c:83`, `mmc.c:120`, `mmc.h:32/38/141/142/149`,
# `mmc_ops.c:203/214/224/296/301` and the `sdhci.c:1165-1172` / `1169-1175` ranges were re-measured and are
# correct as written. **`check_line_citations.py` could not have caught any of them**: it reports
# **NOT-IN-REPO 165** of the 237 citations in the live arm's own prose, because `external/` is untracked and
# `git` - the baseline 808 chose for has-moved - has no answer there. Recorded, not closed.
# 810 section 8 said the check does not collapse the six into one; it makes them agree and keeps them
# agreeing. This rung is the first one that had to live under that rule, and it did.
#
# (7) CONTAINMENT, MEASURED ON ALL ELEVEN MEMBERS OF THE RUNG BELOW. The value-33 build from THIS SAME
# SOURCE is compared member by member against the SPENT rung-34 park
# `out/stage90/frozen/armed-storage-7341f5f6/`; the measurement is in the arm's own experiment doc and
# its result is that the ENTRY IMAGE REPRODUCES BYTE FOR BYTE at value 33 - `xnu_arm_entry.bin`
# `7341f5f6...`, `xnu_arm_entry.elf` `6f66302f...`, `stage90-qcdt.img` `b9319777...` and the rest - and
# the only member that moves is `xnu_arm_entry-sources.txt`, which records sources BY CONTENT and must
# move when the two tools are edited: EXACTLY THREE OF ITS 23 LINES (its own artifact-hash line plus
# `build_entry.sh` and `entry_storage.c`, the two files this step touched), with the file set identical
# at 23 files. **That is the same measurement 804 made one rung down, and it is what licenses the
# `>= 34` guard as a claim about the artefact rather than about the text.**
#
# (8) THE ARM'S OWN DELTA, MEASURED AGAINST THE SPENT RUNG-34 PARK:
#   `xnu_arm_entry.bin`      5,552,764 -> 5,552,764   SIZE UNCHANGED; `7341f5f6...` -> `6cd6fae8...`
#   `xnu_arm_entry.elf`      6,732,528 -> 6,732,556   +28 bytes, and the gate's symbol addresses move
#                                                     with the .text that grew
#   `xnu_arm_entry-config.txt`  TWO LINES move: the artifact's own hash, and STORAGE_PROBE 33 -> 34
#   `stage90-build-config.txt` BYTE-IDENTICAL to rungs 23 through 34 (`6c2b6038...`) - NO payload
#                              switch moved, so the payload rebuild is the embedded entry image alone
#   `stage90_fixture.macho`   BYTE-IDENTICAL (`52bc9c35...`) - unchanged from every rung since it was added
#   `stage90-qcdt.img`        8,572,928 -> 8,572,928   `b9319777...` -> `e0a1cb5c...` - the wrapper the
#                              press sends, which embeds the entry image and therefore must move
#   `stage90.bin` / `.elf` / `.img` and `SHA256SUMS.txt`: all move for that same reason and no other
#   containment on this build: TWO OF ELEVEN members byte-identical to the spent rung-34 park
#                              (`stage90-build-config.txt`, `stage90_fixture.macho`), and the nine that
#                              moved are the eight that embed or list the entry image plus the entry
#                              image's own four files - which is why (7)'s value-33 build is the
#                              containment measurement that means anything here
#
# (9) AND THE ARM IS REPRODUCIBLE: the value-34 build was run TWICE - once before `xnu_entry_812` was
# added, once after - and BOTH RUNS' ENTRY ARTEFACTS ARE BYTE-IDENTICAL (`6cd6fae8...`, `8d77ff3a...`),
# so the clause is a READING and not a change: **a build clause that moved the bytes it reads would be
# the defect it exists to catch, one level up.**
#
# (10) WHAT THE PRESS WOULD DECIDE, AND EACH OUTCOME NAMES THE NEXT ACT. `_csd_complete = 1` with
# `_csd_structure = 1` and `_csd_mmca_vsn = 4` is a CSD, and CMD7 is next in the driver's order;
# `_csd_complete = 1` with a structure that is neither 1 nor 2 is a 136-bit register read that is NOT a
# response to this command (738's stale-word trap, one rung up); `_csd_complete = 0` with
# `_csd_pre_state = 2` puts the wall where the measurement already points - **CMD3 has never been
# accepted**, so the card is in IDENT and CMD9 is a command it is not obliged to answer. **THE THIRD IS
# THE ROW THIS ARM'S OWN EVIDENCE PREDICTS**, and the arm is built to say so out loud rather than to
# report it as a failure of CMD9.
#
# THE PRESS IS THE OPERATOR'S AND NO PRESS IS SPENT OR AUTHORIZED HERE. The arm is ARMED AND NOT
# PRESSED, its park is `out/stage90/frozen/armed-storage-6cd6fae8/` with eleven members verified in
# place, and the press path is the two commands `tools/verify_press_ready.sh` prints, run by hand once
# each. **THE GOAL IS NOT MET BY THIS ARM**: no transfer completes, no filesystem is reached and no
# mount is made, so 「让os可以正常启动并且挂载存储」 is not reached and **TWRP-TO-STORAGE STAYS
# WITHHELD**.
#
